**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# c2_qw_n500 重复进程问题（已解决）\\n\\n## 状态: ✅ 已解决 (2026-05-15 00:25 UTC)\\n\\n重复进程 PID 132389/132392 已确认死亡。当前只有原始进程 PID 131864/132002 在运行。\\n\\n## 历史\\n2026-05-14 21:57 CST 发现 GPU2/3 各有 2 个重复进程写同一输出目录。建议杀掉重复 PID 保留原始 tmux 进程。Session #7 确认重复 PID 已自然退出。\\n\\n## c2_qw_n500 数据完整性\\n重复进程运行期间（~8/500 → ~93/500 s015）的输出可能有交叉写入。最终分析时需验证 profiling_results.json 数据一致性（检查时间戳单调性、问题数 =500）。\\n\\n# c3_llama 与 c2_qw_n500 GPU 共享\\n\\nc3_llama 和 c2_qw_n500 在 cuda:2/3 上共享运行。96GB 显存足够两个 7B 模型共存（实测 ~60GB/GPU）。两者均正常推进，无 OOM 或冲突。c3_llama 完成后 GPU 负载将下降，c2_qw_n500 速率可能提升。\\n\\n# 实验速率参考\\n\\n- c2_qw_n500 (Qwen-7B, n=500, cuda:2/3 共享): ~1 min/problem\\n- c3_llama (LLaMA-8B, n=50, cuda:2/3 共享): ~30 sec/problem (s015/s020 实测)\\n- c2_n500_deepseek (DeepSeek-7B, n=500, cuda:0+1 独占): spec 预估 20h 总时长\\n\\n# Qwen s015 边缘性预注册\\n\\nD037/D038: Qwen s015 的 Cohen's d=0.28, CI 含 0。已预注册：若 n=500 后仍不显著，叙事改为 \\\"compound effect emerges above a threshold sparsity level (~s020)\\\"。这不是 negative，是 nuanced finding。\\n\\n# 模型量级差异（非 bug）\\n\\nDeepSeek compound severity magnitude ~4× Qwen（如 s030: DeepSeek d=9.94 vs Qwen d=2.62）。这是 model-dependent onset threshold，不是实验错误。报告时不要直接比较绝对值，比较趋势（ρ=1.0）。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1093, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — Phase-Gated Compound Sparsity\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-14 (D052 审稿应对调整) | 维护者: Director\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。C3 crossover 现象的 LLaMA 架构验证已列入计划（plan_012 pilot-first），以测试发现的跨架构泛化性。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across architectures | pilot_pending | Pilot-first: 2h oracle + heuristic (token position ratio as phase proxy) on LLaMA-8B, s025/s030. Gate: recover >30% compound degradation → full rollout; else → future work. Must include heuristic baseline (oracle upper bound + simple heuristic lower bound). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | planned | pilot: oracle + heuristic (token position ratio) on s025/s030, >30% recovery threshold |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | planned | c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B) |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: Cross-model replication (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) 控制架构变量、隔离训练方式影响（distillation vs instruction tuning）。注意：两模型共享 Qwen2ForCausalLM 架构，不能 claim 跨架构泛化。MATH-500 是 standard benchmark。Limitations 必须诚实声明：(1) 同架构不同训练，不是跨架构；(2) 7B scale only；(3) 单 benchmark。机制解释 (discrimination collapse) 原则上 architecture-agnostic，但未实证验证。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (Qwen cross-model). 若需加强可扩展到不同架构（如 LLaMA/Mistral），但 11 天 deadline 内不现实\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot-first 策略：先 2h 在 s025/s030 上测 oracle upper bound + simple heuristic (token position ratio as phase proxy)。Oracle 回收 >30% compound degradation 才全面铺开，否则降为 future work\\n  - Heuristic baseline 必须包含：oracle 上界 + simple heuristic 下界，compute-matched\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验（mitigation pilot 见 C5b / plan_011）\\n\\n### RC4: St\\n\\n... Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\\\n\\\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\\\n\\\\n## Narrative Arc\\\\n\\\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。C3 crossover 现象的 LLaMA 架构验证已列入计划（plan_012 pilot-first），以测试发现的跨架构泛化性。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\\\n\\\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims\\\\n\\\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |\\\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across architectures | pilot_pending | Pilot-first: 2h oracle + heuristic (token position ratio as phase proxy) on LLaMA-8B, s025/s030. Gate: recover >30% compound degradation → full rollout; else → future work. Must include heuristic baseline (oracle upper bound + simple heuristic lower bound). |\\\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | planned | pilot: oracle + heuristic (token position ratio) on s025/s030, >30% recovery threshold |\\\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | planned | c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B) |\\\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. D005\\n- id: D007\\n  date: 2026-05-13 20:20 UTC\\n  summary: M1 结果无效（KV bug + KDE 失败）：两路并行——P0 修复重跑 + 替代 segmentation 开发\\n  rationale: KV eviction 的 per-layer mask 交集 bug 导致全 NaN（P0），KDE 82% 样本找不到 tri-modal\\n    结构（P1）。但积极信号存在：execution phase 对 head pruning 敏感度 474× vs reasoning 14×，说明 token\\n    级结构性差异真实存在。决策：(A) P0 修复后重跑 M1 获取有效 II + 诊断数据，(B) 并行开发 GMM+BIC / quantile-based\\n    / multi-layer 替代 segmentation。先看重跑 II 是否在现有 3 phase 间有差异，差异显著则 KDE 虽粗糙但假设成立；无差异则上替代方案。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_interaction_index_profiling\\n    role: failed_run\\n    result: KV NaN + KDE 82% fallback + 90% transition\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n- id: D008\\n  date: 2026-05-13 21:23 UTC\\n  summary: M1v2 假设未获支持但测量方法有问题——M1v3 最后诊断：降 sparsity + quantile segmentation + NaN\\n    guard\\n  rationale: M1v2 所有 phase 均 super-additive、无 sign reversal，但 3 个方法论问题使结论不可靠：(1) 30%\\n    random masking 导致 24-33× PPL 退化，超出安全区；(2) KDE 90% fallback，phase 划分无意义；(3) NaN\\n    CI artifact 导致虚假 PASS。M1v3 修复这三点后做最终判定。叙事 pivot：即使全 super-additive，magnitude 跨\\n    segment 差异（+17.8 vs +3.1）仍支持 phase-aware allocation。Pass = magnitude CI 不重叠；Fail\\n    = magnitude 无差异。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_ii_profiling_v2\\n    role: diagnostic\\n    result: All super-additive, no sign reversal, NaN artifact PASS, KDE 90% fallback\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D007\\n- id: D009\\n  date: 2026-05-13 22:44 UTC\\n  summary: Random masking 系统性混淆——v1-v3 测了错误条件；importance-based masking 是正确 MVP 测试，立即启动\\n    v4\\n  rationale: 三次 MVP 实验全用 random masking（torch.randperm 选 heads/KV），天然产生 super-additive\\n    interaction（随机移除组件造成相关性损伤）。初步 importance-vs-random 对比已显示 masking 方法是 II 符号的主导因子（importance\\n    II=-0.91 sub-additive vs random II=+9.38 super-additive）。v3 在 clean methodology\\n    下仍全 super-additive 是预期结果，不代表 PACS 假设无效。真正的 MVP 判定需在 importance-based masking 条件下测试\\n    per-phase II——这才是实际部署条件下的 compound sparsity 行为。v3 high vs mid 在 s=0.2 下 CI 几乎不重叠（[1.06,3.55]\\n    vs [-1.06,1.30]），暗示 importance-based 下 magnitude 差异可能更显著。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_interaction_index_profiling\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: failed (bugs), but importance-vs-random preliminary data is key signal\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D008\\n- id: D010\\n  date: 2026-05-13 22:44 UTC\\n  summary: MVP FAIL on phase-dependent reversal; PIVOT to strategy-dependent direction\\n    reversal as core novelty\\n  rationale: 三轮 MVP 实验（v1 bug/v2 negative/v3 negative）确认 phase-dependent direction\\n    reversal 不存在——所有 phase 均 super-additive，v2 的 5.8x magnitude 差异是 KDE 不均衡 artifact。但\\n    importance vs random masking 数据发现 strategy-dependent direction reversal：random\\n    30% II=+9.38（super-additive）vs importance 30% II=-0.91（sub-additive），20% 也有符号反转。这是更干净、更强的\\n    novelty 锚点，直接 motivate learned compound controller。Pivot 方向：以 strategy-dependent\\n    interaction reversal 为核心 claim，phase conditioning 降为可选的二级贡献。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  -
